2301.TT 光寶科 
 AI 加持獲利創高，2H26 很樂觀

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
光寶科成立於 1975 年，致力於發展光電元件及電子關鍵零組件技術，為台灣第二大
電源供應器廠商，所提供產品應用於電腦、通訊、消費性電子、汽車電子、LED 照明、
雲端運算、工業自動化等領域。    
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 270 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，270 
【大盤指數/股價】43120 / 209

➢ AI 需求旺，2Q26 毛利率表現亮眼、EPS 創單季新高 
光寶科 2Q26 營收 527 億元 ，QoQ+21.4%，YoY+30.4%； 毛利率27.2%，
QoQ+5.6ppts ， YoY+5.1ppts ； 營 益 率 15.6% ， QoQ+6.1ppts ，
YoY+6.3ppts；EPS 為 3.14 元，營收、獲利優於投研部預期。光寶科
2Q26 三大事業部門營收 、 營業利益皆為季增 ， 尤其是雲端物聯網 ， 營收
YoY 增幅達 70%，為營收最大動能；2Q26 毛利率大幅增加，主要是受
惠於新一代 AI 伺服器需求強勁、1Q26 高階電源產品遞延到 2Q26 出
貨、規模經濟效益擴大、智能製造流程優化所致；毛利率上升，連帶提
升營益率、淨利率表現，最終 2Q26 EPS 創單季歷史新高。 
➢ 2H26 AI 新品出貨，展望樂觀 
2H26 隨 GPU 與 ASIC AI 伺服器新品問世，光寶科業績展望樂觀：1) 
110kw power shelf、8.5kw PSU 以及 BBU 逐步放量 ；2) 800V DC Power 
rack 主要是應用於 2027 年的 Kyber 架構，預計於 8 月送交 ASIC 客戶
驗證 ， 有機會在4Q26 進入量產 ，1Q27 量才會比較明顯 ；3) 2.5kW DCDC 
Power Brick 2H26 進入量產。此外，光寶科原本為美系前 5 大 CSP 其
中 3 家的供應商， 公司宣布打入另外2 家 CSP 的供應鏈 ，已有少量產品
交付給客戶。投研部預估光寶科 3Q26 營收 568.2 億元，QoQ+7.8%，
YoY+26.6%，毛利率 24.9%，EPS 為 2.77 元。  
➢ 斥資 11 億元，入股新加坡光通訊元件廠 DenseLight  
DenseLight Photonics 為磷化銦(InP)光通訊元件廠，是具備 InP 磊晶
設計、晶圓製程、雷射元件到封裝測試完整 IDM 的廠商，產品之研發、
製造都在新加坡進行 。 光寶科投資3,430 萬美元取得 DenseLight 約 21%
股權及 1 席董事席次。光寶科本身具備光測試與封裝的能力，擁有光耦
合器產品線，目標是要發展 1.6T / 3.2T 光引擎+光模組，預計未來會將
DenseLight 之晶片放入光寶科的光模組中。 
➢ 維持買進建議，目標價 270 元 
光寶科 2Q26 財報表現亮眼，獲利創單季新高，2H26 展望樂觀，新品
110kW Power shelf、8.5kw PSU 以及 BBU 放量出貨，預期 2026 年 AI
營收可以達到 30%，800V DC Power rack 主要會在 2027 年出貨。公
司亦持續擴產，並且布局光通訊產品(入股 DenseLight)，搶攻 AI 商機。
光寶科歷史 PE 區間為 10X~28X，長線正向展望不變，目前 PE 評價偏
低，建議買進，目標價 270 元(2027F PE 20X)。

AI 需求旺，2Q26 毛利率表現亮眼、EPS 創單季新高 
光寶科 2Q26 營收 527 億元， QoQ+21.4% ， YoY+30.4%；毛利率 27.2% ，
QoQ+5.6ppts，YoY+5.1ppts；營益率 15.6%，QoQ+6.1ppts，YoY+6.3ppts；EPS
為 3.14 元，營收、獲利優於投研部預期。光寶科2Q26 三大事業部門營收、營業利益
皆為季增，尤其是雲端物聯網，營收 YoY 增幅達 70%，為營收最大動能；2Q26 毛利
率大幅增加，主要是受惠於新一代 AI 伺服器需求強勁、1Q26 高階電源產品遞延到
2Q26 出貨、規模經濟效益擴大、智能製造流程優化所致；毛利率上升，連帶提升營益
率、淨利率表現，最終 2Q26 EPS 創單季歷史新高。關於關鍵零組件缺料問題，公司
已經在 2Q26 啟動策略性備料，2H26 料況無虞，新品定價會將材料成本上漲的問題
納入考量。

2H26 AI 新品出貨，展望非常樂觀 
2H26 隨 GPU 與 ASIC AI 伺服器新品問世，光寶科業績展望樂觀：1) 110kw power 
shelf、8.5kw PSU 以及 BBU 逐步放量；2) 800V DC Power rack 主要是應用於 2027
年的 Kyber 架構(Vera Rubin 不需使用 800V DC)，預計於 8 月送交 ASIC 客戶驗證，
有機會在 4Q26 進入量產 ，1Q27 量才會比較明顯 ；3) 2.5kW DCDC Power Brick 2H26
進入量產。此外，光寶科原本為美系前 5 大 CSP 其中 3 家的供應商，公司宣布打入另
外 2 家的供應鏈，已有少量產品交付給客戶。

資訊與消費性電子(IT/CE) 光電產品 雲端及物聯網

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
低軌衛星方面，因客戶積極發射衛星，2026 低軌衛星電源將有倍數成長(低軌衛星在
IT 事業中占比個位數%)。IT/CE 方面，整體市場需求不佳，2H26 表現將弱於 1H26，
不過，零組件成本上升使 IT/CE 產品售價上漲，故光寶科預估 3Q26 IT/CE 營收 QoQ
可持平。投研部預估光寶科 3Q26 營收 568.2 億元，QoQ+7.8%，YoY+26.6%，毛利
率 24.9%，EPS 為 2.77 元。 
圖 2：光寶科 2026 年 AI 電源產品規劃

資料來源：光寶科 
2026 年 CAPEX 再拉升至 180 億元 
光寶科產能位於中國、台灣、越南、泰國、墨西哥、美國，產能規劃為：1)中國廠區生
產伺服器電源/BBU/網通電源/光電半導體/消費性電子/車用電子產品；2)泰國廠生產
光電產品；3)越南廠以安防/網通為主，逐漸增加 PC 電源與鍵盤產品；4)高雄廠以高
階電源為主，2025 年總計有 6 條產線；5)美國德州 Plano 廠已建立 EV charger 、充
電櫃、BBU 產線。 
光寶科董事會於：1) 3/24 決議，於 2 億普通股額度內，視市況辦理 GDR 或私募，預
計籌資逾 240 億元；2) 4/29 董事會當中通過，加碼投資美國 9.19 億美元(約 290 億
台幣)，用於土地、廠房、設備建置；投資越南 1.49 億美元(約 47 億台幣)，用於擴充
現有產能。光寶科本次宣布，提升 2026 年 CAPEX 至 180 億元(前次為 130 億元)，
180 億元當中，70%用於設備+技術的投資、30%用於廠房建置與產線擴充。2026 年
度 CAPEX 支出金額龐大，公司預期 2027 年 CAPEX 會下降。 
斥資 11 億元，入股新加坡光通訊元件廠 DenseLight 
DenseLight Photonics 為磷化銦(InP)光通訊元件廠 ， 該公司發展光通技術已有20 年，
是具備 InP 磊晶設計、晶圓製程、雷射元件到封裝測試完整 IDM 的廠商，產品之研
發、製造都在新加坡進行。光寶科投資 3,430 萬美元 (約新台幣 11.08 億元)取得
DenseLight 約 21%股權及 1 席董事席次。 
光寶科本身具備光測試與封裝的能力，擁有光耦合器產品線，目標是要發展1.6T/3.2T
光引擎 + 光 模 組 ， 預 計 未 來 會 將DenseLight 之 晶 片 放 入 光 寶 科 的 光 模 組 中 。
DenseLight 目前量產出貨的雷射產品功率約為 100mW， 未來會朝200mW~400mW
產品進行開發。雙方合作對於光寶科的獲利貢獻，預計將於 2H27~2028 年顯現。

維持買進建議，目標價 270 元 
光寶科 2Q26 財報表現亮眼，獲利創單季新高，2H26 展望樂觀，新品 110kW Power 
shelf、8.5kw PSU 以及 BBU 放量出貨，預期 2026 年 AI 營收可以達到 30%。800V 
DC Power rack 則預計 3Q26 驗證，主要在 2027 年出貨。公司亦持續擴產，並且布
局光通訊產品(入股 DenseLight)，搶攻 AI 商機。光寶科歷史 PE 區間為 10X~28X，
長線正向展望不變，目前 PE 評價偏低，建議買進，目標價 270 元(2027F PE 20X)。

2Q26 本業獲利 52 億元，年增 139%，符合預期 
2Q26 本業獲利 52 億元，季增 9%，年增 139%，符合預期，主因 AI Power 
Testing 需求強勁，帶動 ATS 營收季增 14%。展望 3Q26，在 1) 電源供應
廠上修 CAPEX 加上 ESS 陸續開始出貨；2) AI GPU  & ASIC 客戶新產品備
貨；與 3) 中美系光通訊大廠仍積極布建 Burn in 產能帶動 ATS 及 Semi 比重
達 93%產品組合轉佳下，本中心預期 3Q26 營收將季持平/年增 112%至 136
億元，毛利率季/年增 3.5 / 4.2 個百分點至 64%，EPS 11.3 元，年減 5%。 
管理層正向看待 2H26 Semi&Photonics 成為主要成長動能 
本次法說會主要重點：1) 1H26 ATS 業務年增 136%，其中 Power testing 
中 AI 佔比已達到 60%，HVDC 採用 400/800V 對公司的測試設備並無影響；
2) 2H26 的營運表現將優於 1H26，主要由 SLT、光電測試、CPO 帶動；3) 
SLT 訂單下半年有來自 GPU/CPU/Networking 需求；4) 公司在各個產品線
均有新品在 2H26 出貨。預估致茂 2026/27 營收將年增 86%/47%，EPS 年
增 59%/55% 至 43.9/68.1 元 ， 其 中 預 估 1) Semi & Photo 業 務 年 增
122%/88% 與 2) Power Testing 設備帶動 ATS 營收年增 103%/31%。 
CPO 開始出貨且 SLT TAM 擴大，給予目標價 2,400 元 
預估 2027 年營收將年增 47%至 775 億元，EPS 達 68.1 元，主要由既有客
戶推論 /代理相關晶片測試需求帶動的 SLT TAM 擴大 、CPO Tester 及
CoPoS Metrology 進入量產拉貨期、及 HVDC 滲透率提升所帶動。目前公
司股價交易於 2027 年 EPS 的 28 倍本益比，雖落於過去十年本益比區間
(8x-35x)的中上緣，不過本中心持續看好 1) 未來 CPO 及 CoPoS 新產能建
置進入設備拉貨高速成長周期；2) 晶片和 AI Server Rack 功耗上升帶動測
試時間以及既有 SLT/Power testing 設備的需求自然增長；與 3) SLT 、
Burn-in、與 AI Power SST 新測試設備持續拓展新市場之潛在成長動能，因
此我們基於 2027 年的 EPS 68.1 元並給予公司過去十年評價上緣以及與測
試產業領導公司愛得萬相同的目標本益比 35x，得出合理目標價 2,400 元。 
元大觀點 
⧫  2Q26 本業獲利符合預期，主因 AI Power Testing 業務持續成長。 
⧫  
展望 2027 年，預估致茂營收將年增 47%，主因既有 SLT TAM 擴大，
CPO 及 CoPoS 進入量產拉貨期、及 HVDC 滲透率提升所帶動 
⧫  
看好 1) CPO 及 CoPoS 2027 進入成長期；2) AI 功耗上升帶動設備需
求自然增長；與 3)新測試設備拓展新市場之潛能，給予公司過去十年
評價上緣的目標本益比 35x，得出合理目標價 2,400 元。

### Page 2

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 7 月 31 日 Chroma 第2 頁，共 14頁  
營運分析 
2Q26 本業符合預期，主因 AI Power Testing 業務優於預期 
2Q26 本業獲利 52.4 億元，季增 9%，年增 139%，符合市場預期。以各業務別來看： 
⚫ ATS 業務營收 53.9 億元，季增 14%，年增 128%，主因 AI Power Testing 需求強勁。 
⚫ Semi/Photonics 業務營收 43.9 億，季增 29%，年增 99%，其中半導體業務季增動能來自於
AI GPU 晶片客戶 SLT 拉貨 ; Photonics 主要來自於光模組(Transceiver) Burn in 設備貢獻。 
⚫ Oversea 業務營收 22.9 億元，季減 12%，年增 110%，年增主因受惠主要晶圓廠退出成熟製
程後對中國成熟製程廠商的外溢效果。 
在機台零件、記憶體、及被動元件等成本上升抵銷 Power Testing 及 Semi&Photonic 等高毛利產品
出貨成長之下，毛利率季減 2.1%/年減 4.9%至 60.5%，低於市場預期 2.6 個百分點; 本業獲利達
52.4 億元，年增 139%，大致符合市場預期 ; EPS 12 .15 元，年增 164%，優於市場預期 13%，主
因業外有處分股權以及資產獲利 12.7 億元，約為 EPS 3 元。 
展望 3Q26，在 1) 電源供應廠(如：台達電、光寶科與 Flex 等)上修 CAPEX 更積極布建產能以及對
應的 Power testing 產能，加上 ESS 陸續開始出貨；2) AI GPU&ASIC 客戶新產品進入量產前設備
備貨；與 3) 中系與美系光通訊大廠仍積極布建 Burn in 產能帶動 ATS 及 Semi 比種達 93%產品組
合轉佳下，本中心預期 3Q26 營收將季持平/年增 112%至 136 億元，毛利率季/年增 3.5 / 4.2 個百
分點至 64%，EPS 11.3 元，年減 5%。

預期 2027 年 CPO Insertion3/4 之營收將貢獻 2027 年營收 16% 
致茂公司於此次法說會預期 2H26 將開始出貨 Insertion 4O CPO tester 產品予客戶，呼應近期同業
Formfactor 於法說會說明 Insertion 1 相關營收將在 3Q26 底超越公司原先預設的 10-20M $USD 營
收，2026 年全年 CPO 營收將顯著超過 20M $USD 之水準。預期致茂之 58721 I3 測試機台將取代
原先 RD 機台的中國供應廠商成為 Insertion 3 站點主要供應商，且加上 Insertion 4 O 之營收貢獻，
預估 2027 年相關營收將達 95 億元。此外，因 Transceiver 客戶在中國產能持續擴增、美係 Laser 
diode 客戶產能持續提升、且產品快速規格往 800G/1.6T 推進下，將帶動光學晶片 Burn in 測試需
求在 2026 年翻倍成長的高基期下於 2027 年保有 30%成長，挹注整體 2026 年 Photonics 營收將年
增 188%至 33 億元，2027 年亦將年增 281%至 125 億元。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 7 月 31 日 Chroma 第4 頁，共 14頁  
展望 2026 年，預估致茂營收將年增 82%，主要動能來自 AI Power 、GPU/ASIC
之 SLT、與 Photonics 業務設備需求 
本次法說會，主要重點主要有四： 
1. 1H26 ATS 業務年增 136%，其中 Power testing 中 AI 佔比已達到 60%，HVDC 採用
400/800V 對公司的測試設備並無影響。 
2. 2H26 的營運表現將優於 1H26，主要由 SLT、光電測試、CPO 帶動 
3. SLT 訂單下半年有來自 GPU/CPU/Networking 需求 
4. 公司在各個產品線均有新產品預計在 2H26 出貨並貢獻營收，其中半導體包含面板級封裝
Metrology 機台出貨，處於量產前調整/試用階段、Burn-in 機台已收到訂單；光電測試業務包
含 I3/4 CPO 測試機+雷射晶片預燒機台；電源測試業務包含回收式市電模擬電源、BBU 測試
方案、以及高並聯數 (60x)電源負載測試機 。Metrology 從去年開始，除了主要晶圓代工廠，
也從 OSAT 端看到更多需求。 
預估致茂 2026 營收將年增 86%至 527 億元，EPS 年增 59%至 43.9 元，主要動能將來自： 
1) Rubin SLT 設備隨晶片量產持續出貨，AMD 測試規格提升。 
Rubin 晶片功耗將達 1.8~2.3kw，為 Blackwell B200 晶片之 1.3~1.6 倍，在 SLT 階段之測試機台也
將隨晶片世代推進，提升 1) 測試散熱系統、2) SLT 模擬真實測試環境的變更、以及 3) 新增額外佔
點，如預燒或是檢測等等 ，使換機需求維持在穩定的 1-2 年週期。同時，預期在先進封裝(2.5/3D)
整合更多待測物增加測試複雜度、測試製程中的溫度控制、晶片本身功能提升等因素影響下， 測試
時間也將隨 TDP 持續成長。根據本中心預估 Rubin 系列之 SLT 機台 TAM 將較 Blackwell 成長 1 倍。
此外 AMD MI 400 系列在晶片 TDP 將與 Rubin 同步下，預期訂單規模亦將提升至 Blackwell 水平。
再加上 ASIC SLT 持續貢獻以及新客戶推論/代理 AI 晶片的 CPU/LPU 2026 開始小量挹注下，2027
年放量下，預估 2026/27 年 SLT 業務營收將年增 105%/63%。

圖 4：2026 年 SLT 業務營收將年增 105% 圖 5：2026 年 SLT 客戶營收分布預估

資料來源：元大投顧預估 資料來源：元大投顧預估

2) Metrology 被導入於先進封裝新產線，2026/2027 年將逐步放量。 
致茂之 Metrology 設備可再分為 Probe Mark ( 型號 7980)以及 Metrology ( 型號 7981)，預估在
2026 年將逐步導入於國內晶圓代工廠/OSAT 先進封裝之新建產線。2026/2027 年 Metrology 業務，
將主要隨 CoWoS 產能擴增成長，並預期在 2H27 年開始有 COPOS 機台 799X 系列的量產訂單挹
注並在 2028 迎來量產訂單的成長期。預估 2026/27 Metrology 設備業務預估將年增 218%/10%。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 7 月 31 日 Chroma 第5 頁，共 14頁

年增 100%/44%

資料來源：元大投顧預估 資料來源：元大投顧預估 
3) Power Testing 設備隨大客戶如台達電、光寶科擴產帶動，預估 2026/27 年 ATS 營收將年增
93%/31% 
資料中心電源架構在總體 AI 晶片功耗以及單櫃功耗快速提升下，升級的項目主要包含 1）電源模組
的功率提升以及 2）400V/800V 高壓直流電源架構變更兩大趨勢。其中更高功率的 10-30kw PSU 
/100-200kw Power shelf 在測試時將需要並聯更多的電源供應及電子負載測試機，有利致茂發揮其
並聯模組機群的高同步率、能源回收、以及大功率測試產線客製化的優勢。在高壓直流架構升級中，
新增的+-400V/800V 升壓 PSU、DC/DC PSU 、DCDC converter 、BBU 模組亦為致茂帶來全新的
測試需求以及機台升級機會。致茂憑藉可依照客戶 CAPEX 規劃彈性建置的模組化產品線、完整的
測試產線設計服務、以及自營通過 ISO 19251 的機台維修校正中心，在主要電源供應商如台達電、
光寶科、Flex 中市佔率超過 60%。預期在 power testing 穩定貢獻以及 ESS 陸續出貨下 2H26 ATS
逐季營收仍將維持 50%以上的年增率，並在 2026 年帶動 ATS 業務年增 93%，預計 2027 年隨 AI 
資料中心建置對電力供應需求對持續增加且 HVDC 滲透率提升至約 30%之下，測試設備佔電源供
應商 CAPEX 佔比將持續提升帶動致茂 ATS 業務在 2027 延續 31%的年增率。

圖 8：HVDC 架構下致茂 Power testing 設備具有多項機會

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 7 月 31 日 Chroma 第6 頁，共 14頁  
產業概況  
ATE Tester 設備領先廠商仍以海外為主 
ATE Tester 三大類型及佔比方面： 
⚫ SoC (65 -70%)：測試數位晶片如 CPU、ASIC、GPU、DDIC、RF 晶片等，預估 2023-2026
年銷售 CAGR 達 18%，難度亦最高，Advantest、Teradyne、Cohu 為領導業者。 
⚫ 線性及分離元件 (Linear and Discrete ；5-10%)：測試 PMIC、IGBT、MOSFET 等，預估
2023-2026 年銷售 CAGR 達 9%，難度較低，長川、華峰等多家中國業者已推出相關機台。 
⚫ 記憶體 (Memory；15-20%)：測試 DRAM、NAND 等主記憶體，預估 2023-2026 年銷售
CAGR 達 10%，市場仍由 Advantest、Teradyne 掌握。

先進製程趨勢帶動 ATE/SLT 分選機需求上升 
未來先進製程晶片(AP、GPU 與 ASIC 等)皆有 SLT 需求，SLT 測試優勢在於測試晶片在終端使用
情境下的動態表現，且因為能夠進行並測 (一次可測試多個晶片 )，便能將單顆晶片的測試成本維持
在一定水平。 
相對於 FT 測試，SLT 測試所需元件不須 ATE Tester ，而僅需 Handler 與 Load Board & Socket 、
電腦主機(Host)，而 Host 放入 Handler 下的腔體，並通過 Load Board 上的接口即可運行 1) 相容
性檢測；2) 溫度檢測；3) 預燒測試。目前主要提供廠商包含 Advantest、致茂、鴻勁等。

圖 10：隨晶片複雜度提升，SLT 能有效減少封裝後的測試成本

資料來源：公司資料、TechInsights、元大投顧

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 7 月 31 日 Chroma 第7 頁，共 14頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
CPO 產品即將出貨且 SLT TAM 持續擴大，給予買進評等，目標價 2,400 元  
預估 2026 年營收成長 86%至 527 億元，主要來自 1) AI Server Power Testing 設備需求帶動 ATS
業務營收年增 103%；2) 預期 SLT GPU/CPU 產品與光通訊 Burn in 設備於 2H26 持續帶動公司成
長，使 Semi & Photo 業務年增 122%。預估 2027 年營收將年增 47%至 775 億元，EPS 達 68.1 元，
主要由 既有客戶推論 /代理相關晶片測試需求帶動的 SLT TAM 擴大 、CPO Tester  及 CoPoS 
Metrology 進入量產拉貨期、及 HVDC 滲透率提升的測試需求所帶動。 
目前公司股價交易於 2027 年 EPS 的 28 倍本益比，雖落於過去十年本益比區間(8x-35x)的中上緣，
不過本中心持續看好 1) 未來 CPO 及 CoPoS 新產能建置進入設備拉貨高速成長周期；2) 晶片和 AI 
Server Rack 功耗上升帶動測試時間以及既有 SLT/Power testing 設備的需求自然增長；與 3) SLT 、
Burn-in、與 AI Power SST 新測試設備持續拓展新市場之潛在成長動能，因此我們基於 2027 年的
EPS 68.1 元並給予公司過去十年評價上緣以及與測試產業領導公司愛得萬相同的 目標本益比 35x，
得出合理目標價 2,400 元。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 7 月 31 日 Chroma 第10 頁，共 14頁

公司簡介  
致茂成立於 1984 年，為自動化測試設備之製造廠商，測試產品範圍包含 IC、被動元件、面板模組、汽車用電子設備，近
年亦切入太陽能光電、 LED、鋰電池、動力電池、電動汽車等新能源自動測試解決方案。致茂之廠區主要位於桃園、高雄
與中國深圳。主要客戶包含 Nvidia、AMD、NXP、Panasonic、友達、台達電、光寶科、穩懋 等。而在半導體測試設備領
域，國內外同業包含 Advantest、Teradyne，LCM 檢測設備競爭廠商包括均豪、志聖。

### Page 11

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體設備與耗材 2026 年 7 月 31 日 Chroma 第11 頁，共 14頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 致茂整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於領先的位置，
在電子設備業的公司中排名亦領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 致茂的整體曝險屬於低等水準，優於電子設備行業的平均水準。該子行業的公司通常面臨商業
道德、碳排放和人力資本等重大 ESG 問題的風險。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：致茂在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力優異，顯示出更好的 ESG 風險管理
績效。 致茂之資訊揭露程度高，顯示對投資者和公眾高度負責。有提出方案管理與重大 ESG 問題相關的風險，但在關
鍵領域缺乏政策和計劃，不過並未受到牽連任何重大的 ESG 相關爭議。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

陳鈞皓 C00264@ibfs.com.tw 
2360.TT 致茂 
 半導體測試需求強勁 成長動能無虞

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
致茂電子成立於 1984 年，為專業測試儀器領導廠商，提供測光源、電源、半導體設
備等儀器，並以自有品牌 Chroma 行銷全球。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
強力買進 2,700 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，2,700 
【大盤指數/股價】39933 / 1910

➢ 2Q26 營運優於預期 毛利率呈現季度成長 
回顧 2Q26，量測儀器產品線營收約 61.69 億元，佔母公司營收 56%，
與 1Q26 的 58%差異不大。半導體與光電測試營收為 43.86 億元，佔比
則為 39%，與 1Q26 的 37%差異不大。獲利率方面，毛利率自上一季的
62.6%下滑至本季的 60.5%，主因短期受到 IC、被動元件等原物料漲價
所影響 。 另外不同區域別帶來的產品組合差異也使毛利率受到微幅影響 。
而受惠業外因處分凌華股票及認列員工住宅收入使業外貢獻 12.72 億
元，相較 1Q26 的 2.97 億元大幅提升，帶動 EPS 呈現 QoQ 成長。整體
而言 2Q26 本業符合預期，財報受惠業外貢獻而優於預期。2Q26 營收
135.29 億元， QoQ+14.08% ， YoY+109.58% 。 EPS 12.15 元，
QoQ+32.58%，YoY+162.27%。 
➢ 2H26 成長動能集中半導體 FT/SLT Handler 
1H26 成長動能較大來自於量測儀器產品線，以 2Q26 來說該產品線年
增約 129%。而下半年受惠 GPU、CPU、LPU 等晶片對 SLT 需求浮現，
帶動半導體測試產品線有較強的成長動能。而觀察終端晶片量而言美系
GPU 大廠之晶片數量於 2027 年約在 600~700 萬顆之需求，該公司之
CPU 產品則約 500~600 萬顆需求。該部分需求之 SLT 測試機台已於上
半年陸續貢獻營收。下半年則接續美系 A 客戶於 2027 年的需求，尤其
其 CPU 需求量約達 500~600 萬顆，不亞於 GPU 客戶之晶片出貨量，
帶動下半年半導體測試 SLT 機台出貨力道強勁。 
➢ 未來兩年成長路徑明確 股價仍未高估 
電源測試機台於上半年已大幅成長，下半年則由半導體測試機台接棒，
3Q26~4Q26 則有 CPO 相關測試機台陸續貢獻營收 ，2H26~2027 年成
長動能明確 。 考量國際同業如 Teradyne、Advantest 等測試領導廠商在
同樣高成長性下享有超過 40X 之本益比，研究員預期未來兩年為致茂之
高成長期，應享有較高之評價，並預估 2027 年 EPS 約 63.26 元。考量
以上，維持目標價 2,700 元(2027F PE 42x)，建議強力買進。

個股報告

公司介紹 
致茂電子成立於 1984 年，為專業測試儀器領導廠商，提供測光源、電源、半導體設
備等儀器，並以自有品牌 Chroma 行銷全球。公司產品廣度及深度具一定規模，包括
用於電源供應器 、 變壓器 、 充電裝之電源測試機台 。 用於電源管理IC、RF 晶片 、CPU、
GPU 等分選機。及用於 3D 感測器 CPO 矽光等光電整合測試機台等。以產品營收佔
比分類 ，2Q26 量測及自動化佔母公司比率約 56%、 半導體/矽光測試占母公司比例約
39%。 
圖 1：2Q26 產品營收佔比示意圖

資料來源：公司資料 
2Q26 營運優於預期 毛利率呈現季度成長 
回顧 2Q26，量測儀器產品線營收約 61.69 億元，佔母公司營收 56%，與 1Q26 的
58%差異不大。半導體與光電測試營收為 43.86 億元，佔比則為 39%，與 1Q26 的
37%差異不大。獲利率方面，毛利率自上一季的 62.6%下滑至本季的 60.5%，主因短
期受到 IC、被動元件等原物料漲價所影響。另外不同區域別帶來的產品組合差異也使
毛利率受到微幅影響。而受惠業外因處分凌華股票及認列員工住宅收入使業外貢獻
12.72 億元，相較 1Q26 的 2.97 億元大幅提升，帶動 EPS 呈現 QoQ 成長。整體而言
2Q26 本業符合預期，財報受惠業外貢獻而優於預期。 2Q26 營收 135.29 億元，
QoQ+14.08%，YoY+109.58%。EPS 12.15 元，QoQ+32.58%，YoY+162.27%。 
2H26 成長動能集中半導體 FT/SLT Handler 
1H26 成長動能較大來自於量測儀器產品線，以 2Q26 來說該產品線年增約 129%。
而下半年受惠 GPU、CPU、LPU 等晶片對 SLT 需求浮現，帶動半導體測試產品線有較
強的成長動能。半導體測試主要成長動能來自於 SLT 分選機，SLT 測試透過模擬真實
的軟硬體環境來確認晶片功能的正常性 ， 另外公司也支援ATC 控溫之系統 ， 主因GPU
運算時會產生較高的功耗，透過 ATC 可模擬不同環境下之測試環境。而測試機台龍頭
Teradyne(TER US)及 Advantest(6857 JT)皆表示測試機台市場將有大幅之成長。
Teradyne 在最新的電話會議提到 ， 預估在2030 年 ATE 市場規模將超過 200 億美元 ，
相較 2025 年的 120 億美元有 60%以上的增長幅度，且預期 ATE 佔晶圓廠資本支出
比例自 2023 年的 4%提升至 2026 年的 8%，相較過去的佔比有翻倍成長之表現。而

量測及自動化 半導體/矽光測試 Turnkey 其他

個股報告 
另外也預期 CPO 測試市場將在 2028 年成長至 3~7 億美元左右之規模。Advantest
則預期全球測試設備之 TAM 自 4 月預估的 110~120 億美元大幅上調至 130~145 億
美元。其中 SoC 測試機 TAM 達 105~115 億美元，記憶體測試機則約 25~30 億美
元。兩家測試設備龍頭皆對測試市場具有更正向之展望。而觀察終端晶片量而言 美系
GPU 大廠之晶片數量於 2027 年約在 600~700 萬顆之需求 ， 該公司之CPU 產品則約
500~600 萬顆需求 。 該部分需求之SLT 測試機台已於上半年陸續貢獻營收 。 下半年則
接續美系 A 客戶於 2027 年的需求，尤其其 CPU 需求量約達 500~600 萬顆，不亞於
GPU 客戶之晶片出貨量，帶動下半年半導體測試 SLT 機台出貨力道強勁。 
除此之外在 FT Handler 佈局方面為公司 2026 年成長動能之一 ， 過往即有該產品之製
造經驗，惟過去一段時間專注在 SLT Handler 開發，近期公司有望重回該領域，在整
體 FT Handler 仍處於供不應求的狀況下，有望貢獻一定份額之營收。在電源及半導
體產品線成長之態勢下，研究員預估 3Q26 營收 144.37 億元， QoQ+6.71%，
YoY+125.22%。EPS 11.47 元，QoQ-4.8%，YoY-3.74%。 
2H26 開始 CPO 相關測試機台加入 添增成長動能 
CPO 產品佈局方面，由於光引擎跟電子晶片需要被封裝在一起，在物理特性、對準精
度方面具有較大的挑戰。傳統之晶片測試僅需要透過探針接觸焊墊來讀取電子訊號，
但光訊號無法透過金屬探針做讀取，因此測試設備必須具備雷射光源與光譜儀，並透
過光纖來與晶片耦合才能檢測光學的損耗等。另外在精度方面也有所挑戰，原因為光
訊號之容錯率極低，只要位置偏差一微米，光學耗損就會劇增，因此無法用傳統的機
械對準來對位。為了解決以上問題，CPO 檢測設備同時要有測電及測光之能力，通常
在晶圓頂部與 ATE 對接，並透過探針卡進行電訊號之測試，而在晶圓底部對光纖陣列
進行光學檢測，此種同時進行光電測試的機台難度高，且額外多出了雷射光源、主動
對準系統等，單價將顯著高於傳統半導體用之 ATE 測試機台。目前公司在 CPO 進展
方面以取的多項成果及訂單 ， 其中在Insertion 3 光學引擎測試方面 ， 主要測試發射端
及接收端的功能性，例如測試發射端內部的電是否能成功驅動雷射光及測試光的功率
及訊號品質；或是測試光偵測器接收到光訊號後，其輸出的訊號是否達成客戶要求標
準等等，在該階段公司已取得大量訂單。由於 Insertion 3 在測量的時間較慢，使客戶
對機台需求量大幅增加，而競爭對手產能較低，使公司切入該市場且有機會拿到較大
的市場份額。 
而在 Insertion 4 部分有可分為 4-O 及 4-E 兩大部分 ，4-O 主要用來測試 CPO Switch
封裝後的光品質 ， 例如測量雷射的光功率 、 波長等 。 而4-E 則用來測量電訊號之品質 ，
例如 ASIC 晶片的電壓、電流等參數。目前公司也在該兩階段大有斬獲，成為未來重
要的成長動能。考量 2026~2027 年具多項成長動能，研究員預估 2026 年/2027 年
營收分別為 557.42 億元/754.73 億元，YoY+96.89% /35.4%。EPS 分別為 45.84 元
/63.26 元，YoY+66.24% /38.43%。 
未來兩年成長路徑明確 股價仍未高估 
電源測試機台於上半年已大幅成長，下半年則由半導體測試機台接棒， 3Q26~4Q26
則有 CPO 相關測試機台陸續貢獻營收 ，2H26~2027 年成長動能明確 。考量國際同業
如 Teradyne、Advantest 等測試領導廠商在同樣高成長性下享有超過 40X 之本益比 ，
研究員預期未來兩年為致茂之高成長期，應享有較高之評價，並預估 2027 年 EPS 約
63.26 元。考量以上，維持目標價 2,700 元(2027F PE 42x)，建議強力買進。

EMIB-T under development; optimism about L-T GM; glass substrate still far
away — For EMIB-T, Unimicron and another two Japanese peers are developing
this technology with yield rate target of 50% reached at the first phase. Mass
production timing would be in 2027. Despite investors’ concerns over poor GM
profile for EMIB-T on low yield rate, the company indicated there are guaranteed
minimum GM profiles on low yield. And if higher yield rate could be met, there

would be extra bonus awarded based on the incentive program. The mgmt. also
indicated that she thinks 50-60% GM profile for ABF substrate is a reasonable
range in the mid to long term in this cycle. For glass substrate, the company is
working with multiple customers and expects mass production would not occur
until 2030.
Earnings adjustment; reiterate our Buy rating — We adjust our 2026/27 /28E
earnings to factor in 1) depreciation from Thailand plant and 2) dilution impact from

its recent fundraising. We remain constructive on the ABF sector and reiterate our
Buy rating with unchanged DCF-based TP NT$1,500 (implied 31x 2027E EPS).

### Page 7

Unimicron (3037.TW)
30 July 2026 Citi Research
7
Unimicron
Company description
Established in 1990, Unimicron is the world's No.1 PCB maker and HDI board
maker. After the acquisition of PPT in late 2009, its revenue exposure to
substrate has surpassed 40%, with the rest almost equally shared by
conventional PCB and HDI. Unimicron has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
China (Suzhou, Kunshan, Shenzhen). UMC is the largest shareholder, with a
~13% stake.

Investment strategy
We rate Unimicron shares a Buy. Our positive view is premised on: more
upside potential in the ABF segment thanks to product-mix improvements,
better pricing on more complex product upgrade, and capacity expansion.
Despite macro uncertainty, from a long-term perspective, we believe the
tightness in ABF is unlikely to ease without much new capacity ramped
aggressively in the upcoming years, and Unimicron should be the largest
beneficiary with its scale in ABF. Thanks to AI GPU/ASIC demand, we now see

strong growth in 2026/27E ABF consumption. We believe Unimicron could
see strong revenue growth again as it did in 2019–21 from both ABF new
capacity and a BT rebound.
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,500. We use a 1-year forward-looking
DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect Unimicron's ability to generate
stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 4.3% (10-year government bond rate), a
market risk premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share

price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 13.7%. Our target price is equivalent
to 66x/31x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus
PE multiple of the past five years. However, as the market focuses on 2027
upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI business; 2) significant increase

in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure
from depreciation due to higher capex investments.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

We hosted EMC’s 2Q26 post result call on July 30th. Key 
takeaways include: 
(1) EMC mentioned MP (mass production) for the M9 CCL products 
has started in 3Q26 and the demand will get even stronger in 4Q26 
and beyond, while M9 CCL has already accounted for low single 
digits of total revenue in 1H26, despite limited shipments. 
Management expects Nvidia HGX R200 UBB to be the first board to 
adopt M9 CCL in 3Q26 and further expects shipment of M9 CCL for 
LPU to start from 4Q26. 
(2) Management mentioned the possibility of another round of CCL 
pricing hike to occur in 2H26 as the company continues to see cost 
increases in raw materials. Material supply remains tight in 2H26, 
which spans across glass fiber (E-glass & low-DK2) and high-end 
copper foil (mostly HVLP4). Despite seeing possibility of further cost 
increases in 2H26, the company can pass-through the higher cost 
through price hikes. 
(3) Without considering the potential pricing hike and further M9 
grade CCL demand growth in 3Q26, EMC now sees its 3Q revenue 
minimal growth rate at 15% QoQ and the most conservative 3Q GM 
estimate is 35% +-1.5ppt (1.1ppt GM expansion at the midpoint vs. 
2Q26 GM), while the company also mentioned potential upward 
revision is possible if market conditions continue to be even more 
favorable.  
(4) EMC continue to make progress on substrate CCL, and expects 
the product shipment could start from 4Q26 the earliest with a high 
GM (40-50%+ vs. company ‘s ~34% in 2Q26). The company holds 
1.6% market share in substrate CCL market in 2025 and expects to 
double the share in a one-year time frame; it will expand its 
substrate CCL capacity by 100% in 4Q26 and will expand the 
capacity by another 100% in 2027.

### Page 3

(5) Company mentioned its future capacity expansion has already been fully booked by
end customers and it’s discussing capacity with customers for after 2H28. In terms of
overall capacity expansion plans, on top of the existing +5%/+51% YoY capacity growth
(year-end to year-end basis) in 2026/27, the company plans to further expand
1.2mn/0.6mn sheets of monthly capacity in 1Q28/2Q28 (another 19% increase by mid
2028 vs. 2027 end capacity).
(6) In terms of the high-end CCL competition, management believe the more favorable

competition landscape will be even more favorable going into the long term as CCL
migrates to more advanced generation; they also don’t expect competition from
low-end CCL peers, which claim to be making progress with M10 CCL, will have any
impact on the company. The company believes the high-end CCL shortage issue will
continue for at least the next 2+ years. For M10, EMC has submitted samples for
verification but does not expect the product will be adopted in foreseeable future.

(7) For switches, the company mentioned that 800G switches will remain mainstream in
2026 and start to see shipment for 1.6T switches starting in 3Q26. The company also
sees upgrade in CCL for switches, migrating from M8 grade CCL (low-DK glass fiber with
HVLP3 copper foil) to M8+ grade CCL (low-DK2 glass fiber with HVLP4 copper foil).
Investment view
We maintain our positive view on EMC, due to not only the potential better pricing
outlook with the tightening supply demand conditions on the high-end high quality CCL

supply (see here), but also thanks to the company’s leadership position in the high GM
M9 grade CCL and its good progress on penetrating into the high GM substrate CCL
supply chain, while we believe all three factors above should not only drive the
company’s top line growth in coming years, but also imply an expanding GM/OPM
outlook.
We are positive on EMC’s commentary that its MP on M9 is ongoing with increasing
orders and new models to adopt this high-end product, while we expect the M9 grade

CCL to have the highest GM among companies’ product portfolios (we expect M9 CCL
GM should be 50-55%+ based on current pricing status). Considering EMC’s leadership
position in M9 grade CCL, we expect the company to enjoy a solid ASP expansion in
2027 and beyond considering (1) we expect the M9+ grade CCL demand will grow 2x+
YoY in 2027 and further expand in 2028 and beyond, and (2) M9 grade CCL pricing per
sheet is normally 100%+ higher than M8 grade CCL. We therefore expect the company

will enjoy strong revenue/GM growth in coming quarters/years even without any like for
like base pricing hike.
For substrate CCL, TAM was ~US$1.3bn in 2025 per EMC (TAM is only ~40% of EMC’s
revenue in 2025), and if assuming the substrate CCL TAM growth rate to be in-line with
ABF/BT substrate industry growth rate, the TAM will increase to US$3.4bn/US$5.5bn in
2027/28 (28%/28% of EMC’s 2027/28 per GSe), suggesting a low revenue contribution
even if the company can get a higher market share (was 1.6% in 2025). However, if we

consider the GM of the substrate CCL during supply tightness periods can be as high as
50-70%, this new business can contribute more profitability growth in coming years.
Overall, we reiterate our Buy rating on EMC with an unchanged TP of NT$10,200,
considering its solid position in the AI CCL industry (which should see 100%+ revenue
CAGR in at least the next 3-5 years) and its early penetration into the high margin
30 July 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Elite Material (2383.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

substrate CCL business, as well as the favorable pricing environment now in the AI CCL 
market, which should continue to drive EMC’s profitability in coming quarters/years.  
Valuation 
Maintain Buy on EMC, with an unchanged 12-TP of NT$10,200 (based on the same 27x 
2027E EPS; in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in past 3 years).

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for 
3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
(3) rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$10,200 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

We hosted TUC’s 2Q post result call on July 30th, and key 
takeaways included:  
(1) TUC’s Thailand plant new capacity ramp up timing is 2 months 
earlier than expected (thanks to the faster than expected 
technology ramp up speed), while the company previously believed 
the new capacity will be ramp up from September, but it started the 
capacity ramp up from mid of July. The company expects the new 
Thailand capacity ramp up will improve the company’s market share 
in high-end AI server projects from 3Q.  
(2) TUC announced the new capacity of 1.95mn sheets (~80% of the 
capacity today) in Thailand and mainland China in 2028. The 
company mentioned the new capacity expansion volume is based 
on customers’ actual demand outlook (both US and mainland China 
will have more high-end CCL demand after 2028), and it also 
mentioned the capacity expansion speed is still below customers’ 
order forecast.  
(3) For pricing, the company mentioned most of the pricing hike 
happened in 1H26 was mainly on low end CCL, but it starts seeing 
M7 pricing hike from early 4Q. For M8 grade CCL, the company will 
wait until key Taiwan CCL peer starts raising pricing.  
(4) M7/8 grade CCL already accounted for 39% of total revenue (vs. 
36% in 2Q), and the company further mentioned the M7/8 demand 
will trend up significantly in coming quarters/years, considering the 
increasing adoption on M8 grade CCL from ASIC AI server/ high-end 
switching / future LEO projects.  
(5) For AI server, the company is now working with several 
customers on the future new high-end AI projects qualification 
(mostly M8+ grade CCL) across different GPU/AI CPU/ASIC projects, 
and believes its revenue will continue to trend up in coming quarters 
with more capacity and much better ASP/product mix.

Taiwan Union Technology Corp. (6274.TWO)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

### Page 3

(6) For high-end switching, the company is expecting a solid demand outlook on
800G/1.6T switch adopted with M8/M8.5/M9 grade from existing customers, and it also
been qualified by new switching customers recently which should suggest an even
stronger demand from high-end switching sector. Our supply chain check suggests the
800G/1.6T switch CCL demand should go up by at least 100%+ YoY in 2027, while
management mentioned the growth is in-line with what they see from customers order

forecast.
(7) For LEO, the company mentioned it’s working with key LEO customers now to
support their AI related demand in coming years, and it expects the CCL grade will
continue to upgrade from M6 now to M7/8 in coming quarters/years. Moreover,
customers are asking for more capacity in long term to support their demand after
2028.
(8) TUC believes the ASP/GM uptrend for high-end CCL players just started, and
mentioned the pricing hike on CCL products is not just cost pass through but includes

the additional pricing hike on higher opportunity cost, as it sees a very tight supply on
the high-end high quality CCL capacity. Moreover, the company mentioned the high
quality CCL capacity expansion leadtime is at least 24+ months, and it sees no supply
demand reversal risk at least until end of 2028.
We reiterate our Buy rating on TUC, and expect TUC will continue to improve its product
mix with more high-end spill over orders from EMC (TUC M7/8 accounted for 39% of

total revenue in 2Q vs. EMC at 60-65% in 2Q) from not only the AI server industry, but
also high-end switching and LEO industry, as we expect EMC will only have new capacity
come on stream by end of 2026 (see here) but the M7+ CCL demand will still go up by
50%+ HoH in 2H26 (and we expect the TAM growth will be 100%+ YoY in 2027 - see
here).
Also, we are positive on TUC’s proactive capacity expansion plans in coming years,
considering the solid demand outlook. TUC has seen the most conservative capacity

expansion in the past 10+ years, and it always checks with customers on the future
demand carefully before investing in the new capacity. As a result, we believe TUC’s
proactive capacity expansion strategy this time would benefit from being reviewed with
customers carefully and based on their owned research on the future demand outlook,
suggesting limited risk going into long term.
Moreover, TUC is much more flexible on its pricing strategy vs. EMC, who already did 2

rounds of pricing hike on M2-7 grade CCL since 2Q26, as it continues to see the faster
than expected customers demand outlook, which should help the company to catch up
with EMC’s GM/OPM in coming years. Considering the company’s capacity is fully
utilized now, we believe low end customers could cancel orders due to the high pricing,
which would suggest more capacity/room for TUC to work with high-end/AI customers,
providing scope to improve its ASP and GM (high-end AI project ASP can be 3-5x higher

than low end projects, and the GM can be 40%+ vs. low end only at 5-15%).
Maintain Buy on TUC, with unchanged estimates and 12m TP of NT$3,010, based on
a 22x 2H27-1H28E EPS (+2x STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E
multiple).

### Page 4

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of p roducts for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,010 is based on a 22x 2027E P/E (+2x 
STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).  
Key downside risks:  (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL segment; 
(2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch shipments 
globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

M
Update
Realtek Semiconductor  | Asia Pacific
Better than feared outlook
The stock appears to be a safe harbor vs. other high-flyers (e.g.,
Memory) following the trough into 2Q earnings .
2Q review: 2Q revenue was NT$37.6bn, +3% Q/Q and +18% Y/Y, 3% above MSe. 2Q
gross profit was NT$17.7bn. GM was 47.0%, -2.7ppt Q/Q and -3.2ppt Y/Y, 2.7ppt
below MSe. EPS was NT$7.42, -12% Q/Q and -3% Y/Y, 12% below MSe. Margin
compression was driven by elevated memory pricing and constrained OSAT capacity.

2H26 outlook: PC segment faces demand pressure in 2H26 due to elevated memory
costs and CPU supply tightness, but revenue growth is expected, helped by spec-up
demand. For the consumer segment, TV shipments have begun to soften after FIFA;
home appliance demand continues to grow steadily and global game console
shipments are expected to fall this year. The networking segment demonstrated
demand stability, supported by enterprise infra refresh cycles and the transition

toward higher-value WiFi7 and multi-gig Ethernet solutions. The auto segment is
expected to be seasonally strong in 2H, especially helped by growth from the
European market.
Other highlights: Increased product pricing with certain customers is to take effect
in 3Q, helping GM recover. Realtek is gaining share in the managed switch market,
having increased 2-3ppt vs the previous reporting period. WiFi7 penetration in
notebooks is estimated to reach ~30% this year, while WiFi8 deployment is seen

starting from 1Q28.
Remain EW: The company is indeed showing better than feared outlook due to
FOMP (link). We believe the stock is fairly valued relative to our implied target of
19x 2026E P/E. Supply constraints and weak end-market demand continues, but we
expect the company to manage some revenue growth in 3Q and a continued GM
rebound in 2H, helped by spec-up demand and price increases.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 095: 260730_ubs_delta

### Page 1

ab 30 July 2026
Global Research
Delta Electronics
Outlook intact with HVDC shipping from Q4
Q2 results in-line with latest expectations and milder due to supply constraints
Q226 results were in-line with sales at NT$183.3bn +15% QoQ/+48% YoY driven by
cooling component rush orders driving +22% QoQ/+85% YoY growth to  infrastructure
(34%) while segment OpM  was -180bp to 17.9%. Power electronics (53%) was +13%
QoQ/+48% YoY to NT96.3bn, with OPM of  20.9%. Automation NT$14.3bn, +8% QoQ

/ +11% YoY but reversed to op loss of NT$443mn due to memory inflation, while
mobility NT$8.2bn, +3% QoQ/-26% YoY with NT$744mn op loss. Blended GMs/OpM
declined 140bp/110bp QoQ to 35.6%/16.7% less Q1 benefits from delivered duty on
liquid-cooling products and1x reversals, but booked NT$4.4bn non-op gain and FX to
lift EPS to NT$9.68 above prior UBS/Street NT$9.36/NT$9.58. The margin impact was
less than Delta Thailand as the factory network absorbed component costs/inventory

reserves vs. the parent seeing higher margin system and solution sales.
H226 strong outlook intact, with margins gradually improving
We maintain Q326 +15% QoQ led by GB power supply while liquid cooling could be
slightly weaker given the high base in Q2. We model GMs mildly rebounding from
35.6% to 36.1%/36.7% in Q3/Q4 as the company qualifies 2nd sources and negotiates
to pass through higher costs.  Mix shift to server power supply also should support some

mild margin recovery.  The company stays aggressive on investments, targeting opex
growth of +30% YoY to support heavier system design as power architectures grow
more complex moving toward HVDC/SST and adds more US field support. It also expects
AI demand to sustain next couple years, with capex rising from NT$46.5bn to NT
$67.5bn to expand in Taiwan, Thailand and China for AI and fuel cell investment.
Power racks start initial shipments in Q4 and ramp into ASICs in 2027

The HVDC power racks will start MP in 3Q with small shipments starting  4Q mainly on
+/-400V for ASIC pushing aggressively for next year up to ⅓ of mix but supply
constrained by tight supercapacitors.  The company expects milder ramp for NVIDIA's
800VDC as customers are staying more conservative on adoption. We view the milder
roadmap on Rubin and Rubin ultra staying with the Oberon NVL72 keeps that option
optional for the next 1-2 years. Amid mild HVDC initial ramp, it still stays constructive on

its strong pipeline and push for data center deliveries through 2027 to resolve tight
compute demand even amid the concerns on hyperscaler cash flows.
Estimates intact - Maintain Buy and NT$2,750
With results and outlook in-line with preview, we fine-tune 2026 from NT$43.0 to NT
$42.3 and maintain 2027/28 EPS at NT$67.5/NT$90.  Delta's stock has pulled back 36%
since May due to Kyber delays / less aggressive HVDC ramp, milder Q2 sales / margin
performance and broader AI correction,  pulling it back to 22x/17x 2026-27 EPS.   We

now see better entry with sales ramping in H226, spec upgrades to ASIC power racks
and Rubin at 60% higher TDP over Blackwell and margins holding better than feared.

Company Description
Delta Electronics provides computer switching power supply solutions, as well as video display
and electronics components, for computers, smartphones, telecom and industrial products.
Founded in 1971, the firm has production bases in Taiwan, China, Thailand and India. In

2023, its power electronics, infrastructure and automation segments contributed 62%, 25%
and 13% of revenue, respectively.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's
interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target on PE multiple valuation.

Delta's businesses include SPS and components for a variety of end-applications, video
displays and data networking subsystem and system products. Any changes in these end-
markets could affect our forecasts for Delta. Investment risk could increase/decrease on any
deviation from our expectations for end-market demand and competition.
Downside risks for Delta include: severe supply chain constraints; weaker-than-expected PC/
server demand; a slower-than-expected IA recovery; and a slower ramp of 5G in 2022.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.
EXCEPTIONS AND SPECIAL CASES:UK and European Investment Fund ratings and definitions are: Buy:  Positive on factors
such as structure, management, performance record, discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

## Document 099: 260731_daiwa_Advantech

Source PDF: all report pdf/260731_daiwa_Advantech.pdf

### Page 1

Summary: Advantech’s 2Q26 net profit was TWD4.52bn (EPS: TWD5.2), 32% higher than 
our estimate (TWD3.42bn) and the Bloomberg consensus (TWD3.43bn). 2Q26 revenue of 
TWD26.1bn was higher than our estimate (TWD22.7bn) and the company’s guidance 
range of TWD20.5-21bn, which we attribute to solid industrial demand and ongoing price 
hikes. Per management, they have seen price hikes proliferating into diversified 
components (such as memory and CPU) This also caused the gross margin (37.7%) in 
2Q26 to be lower than its guidance range of 38-40% and our estimate of 39.8%. However, 
operating margin came in stronger at 19.6% (vs. our estimate of 17.7% and street’s 
estimate of 18.2%) due to better operating leverage despite a lower gross margin.

Looking ahead, while the 2Q26 B/B ratio came down QoQ, management remained positive 
on the revenue momentum in 3Q26. In terms of edge AI, Advantech has seen the 
proliferation into different end applications speeding up since 1Q26. Considering the 
blended gross margin from edge AI is at 40-42%, Advantech’s overall gross margin could 
expand along with a rising edge AI contribution. Advantech will host its 2Q26 results call on 
5 August at 14:00 and we will provide updates in due course. We have an Outperform (2) 
rating on this name.

What’s the impact: 
• 2Q26 results first-take: results beat on pull-in demand. Advantech’s 2Q26 net profit 
was TWD4.52bn (EPS: TWD5.2), 32% higher than our estimate (TWD3.42bn) and the 
Bloomberg consensus (TWD3.43bn). 2Q26 revenue of TWD26.1bn was 15% higher 
than our estimate (TWD22.7bn) and the company’s guidance range of TWD20.5-21bn, 
which we attribute to solid industrial demand and ongoing price hikes. Per management, 
they have seen price hikes proliferating into diversified components (such as memory 
and CPU with price increases of 10-20%). This also caused the gross margin (37.7%) in 
2Q26 to be lower than its guidance range of 38-40% and our estimate of 39.8%. 
However, operating margin came in stronger at 19.6% (vs. our estimate of 17.7% and 
street’s estimate of 18.2%) due to better operating leverage despite a lower gross 
margin. Lastly, Advantech’s B/B ratio decreased to 1.44 in 2Q26 (1Q26: 1.77; 4Q25: 
1.33; 3Q25: 1.01).

• Business outlook. Looking ahead, while the 2Q26 B/B ratio came down QoQ, 
management remained positive on the revenue momentum in 3Q26. As for the gross 
margin, the company is still aggressively reviewing its pricing in order to pass through 
additional material costs. As such, the longer-term gross margin range should remain at 
39-40% when the raw material environment stabilises. As for edge AI, the company is

## Document 104: 260731_daiwa_Pegavision

Source PDF: all report pdf/260731_daiwa_Pegavision.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-07-31
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Health Care

What’s new: Pegavision released its 2Q26 results on 31 July after market 
hours. The company maintained a positive view on the Japan market and 
its product roadmap for silicone hydrogel contact lenses in multiple regions, 
including Japan and Europe. We reaffirm our Outperform (2) call.

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat. Pegavision’s 2Q26 revenue of 
TWD2,115m (+12.1% QoQ; +30.0% YoY) beat our estimate and the 
Bloomberg consensus by 14.3% and 1.1%, respectively, mainly due to the 
Japan market in light of its order share gains following vendor 
consolidation. Its gross margin of 53.0% (vs. 53.3% in 1Q26 and 50.9% in 
2Q25) was roughly in line with our estimate (53.0%) and the Bloomberg 
consensus estimate (53.2%). Operating margin of 27.6% (vs. 28.8% in 
1Q26 and 23.3% in 2Q25) beat our estimate (25.5%) but was slightly below 
the Bloomberg consensus estimate (28.2%). Its 2Q26 operating profit of 
TWD585m (+7.7% QoQ; +54.3% YoY) beat our estimate by 23.9% but was 
in line with consensus. Due to some non-op gains and a lower-than-
expected tax rate, its 2Q26 net profit came in at TWD544m (+15.2% QoQ; 
+73.6% YoY), beating our and Bloomberg consensus estimates by 33.8% 
and 10.6%, respectively. Its 2Q26 EPS was TWD7.07. Japan accounted for 
60%+ of its revenue in 1H26 while China accounted for 10%+.

Bright 2H26 revenue; capacity expansion plans in Vietnam and 
Taiwan. Pegavision targets revenue to grow QoQ in 2H26, driven by the 
ramp-up of silicone hydrogel daily contact lenses, which accounted for 10% 
of 2Q26 revenue and is targeted to contribute 15% of 4Q26 revenue, and a 
solid order outlook from the Japan market. In China, Pegavision remains 
cautious on the price war despite its China revenue rising 10% YoY in 
1H26. For 2026-28, Pegavision aims for revenue to grow a double digit % 
YoY vs. its 1H26 revenue growth of 24.2% YoY. It plans to launch its new 
Vietnam plant in 2027. We raise our 2026-27E EPS by 11-18%, mainly on 
our more aggressive revenue forecasts, and introduce our 2028 forecasts.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Outperform (2) rating and raise our 
12M TP to TWD375 (from TWD335), now based on a PER of 14x (from 
15x) on its slightly decelerating earnings momentum, lower than the 
midpoint (16x) of its past-3-year trading range of 10-22x, applied to our 12-
month forward EPS forecast. However, we believe the launch of silicone 
hydrogel contact-lens products will likely be a share-price catalyst, with a 
dividend yield of 3.1-3.8% over 2026-28E to support the share price. 
Downside risks: a macroeconomic slowdown and increased competition.

How we differ: Our 2027E EPS is 8% below Bloomberg consensus, likely 
as we are more conservative on Pegavision’s revenue expansion.

31 July 2026

➢ 2Q26 results beat both Daiwa and Bloomberg estimates

➢ Reaffirming our Outperform (2) rating; raising 12-month TP to TWD375

Company profile 
Founded in 2009, Pegavision is a joint venture between Kinsus and Pegatron focusing on the 
production and sale of soft contact lenses. Pegavision's revenue contribution from OBM (Pegavision) 
was 20% and ODM was 80% in 2025. The company was listed on Taiwan's main board in October 
2019.

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

MediaTek hosted its 2Q26 analyst meeting on 31 July. 3Q26 
guidance came in better than expected, and the company has 
delivered an even more positive message on its AI ASIC business, 
which we believe further reinforces our view that the AI ASIC 
upcycle is just getting started. Management now expects its AI ASIC 
revenue to exceed US$2bn in 2026 (vs. US$2bn previously) and 
raised its 2027 AI ASIC SAM and market share — which now  
implies US$12-$16bn of AI ASIC revenue in 2027 (vs. US$7-$12bn 
previously). Management expressed its confidence in its next-gen AI 
ASIC project, stating that the tapeout is on track, with high 
volume production planned for early 2028.  Packaging and 
substrate yields are also progressing well, which we believe should 
ease investor concerns around execution. Management now expects 
its first AI ASIC project will also contribute in 2028 along with its 
second project, which is expected to enhance the company’s 
market share significantly. With a stronger near- and 
medium-term outlook driven by AI ASIC business, we reiterate our 
Buy rating on MediaTek and tick up our 12m TP to NT$7,000 (25X 
on 2H27-1H28E EPS) from NT$6,800, implying potential upside of 
~97%. 
Higher AI ASIC SAM with higher market share target 
Management now sees its AI ASIC SAM at US$80bn for 2027 (vs. 
US$70-$80bn previously) and raised its targeted market share to 
15%-20% (vs. 10%-15% previously) — implying US$12-$16bn  
potential AI ASIC revenue in 2027. In the near term, the first AI ASIC 
project remains on schedule, with production beginning in 4Q26. 
2026 AI ASIC revenue is now expected to exceed US$2bn (vs. 
US$2bn previously), and revenue is anticipated to scale substantially 
in 2027, driven by increasing customer demand for its favorable cost 
of ownership.  Overall the upbeat AI ASIC guidance reaffirms our 
confidence in MediaTek’s AI ASIC growth outlook, and we slightly 
tick up our 2026E AI ASIC forecast to US$2.2bn (vs. US$2bn

### Page 3

previously), while maintaining our 2027E AI ASIC revenue forecast at US$20.3bn. We
now expect AI ASIC to account for 10%/48% of MediaTek’s revenue in 2026/27E.
Expect significant market share gains in 2028
Management expressed its confidence that, given the strong capability demonstrated by
the first AI ASIC project, its next gen project is on track with market share improving
significantly as the second AI ASIC project ramps — and guided that its first AI ASIC

project will contribute in 2028 alongside its second project, resulting in enhanced
market share. Although management did not provide guidance on the 2028 SAM, it
stated it is confident that the 2028 SAM will be higher than 2027.  Given MediaTek’s
optimistic market share outlook guidance and strong dollar content growth for next-gen
ASIC, we maintain our 2028E ASIC revenue forecast at US$52.5bn, which is expected
to account for 68% of revenue by 2028E.

Next-gen AI ASIC progressing on track
During the meeting, management addressed investor concerns over its
second-generation project in terms of timeline and production yield. On timeline,
management noted that its second ASIC is on track for high-volume production in
early 2028, with design and tapeout schedules progressing on track. On yield,
management stated the yield and reliability of the second AI ASIC project are also on
track, with improving yield at its substrate vendor. In our view, management was more

constructive on the advanced packaging solution relative to the last quarter, and we
believe this should ease investor concerns on potential execution risk on the second
ASIC project.
Gross margin guidance consistent across generations, but we see potential upside
On margins, management maintained that AI ASIC gross margins will be broadly similar
from generation to generation, and slightly dilutive to the corporate average. However,
the business remains operating margin accretive, with the company’s OpEx ratio

expected to decline substantially as revenue and scale become larger into 2027, even as
absolute OpEx rises on continued technology investment. Overall, we see potential
upside to this guidance over time, as MediaTek’s content value increases with each
generation. We believe the second generation chip carries meaningfully higher silicon
content for MediaTek with a larger chip size, deeper design engagement, additional I/O,
and advanced packaging integration - leading to potentially higher gross margin relative

to the first generation.

2Q26 results and 3Q26 guidance recap

3Q26 revenue guidance above GS and Street 
For 3Q26, MediaTek expects revenue to be in a range of NT$152.2-$159.8bn (0% to 5% 
QoQ), gross margin to be within +/- 1.5% of 46%, and OpEx-to-sales to be within +/- 2% 
of 31%. 3Q26 revenue guidance (at the midpoint) came in higher vs. GSe/Bloomberg 
consensus of NT$153.9bn/NT$152.0bn on stronger smart edge expectations. GM 
guidance of 46% +/1.5% was in line vs. GSe/cons. of 46.1%/46.2%. We are now 
modeling 3Q26E revenue to increase by 4.8% QoQ, with GM of 46.3%. By segment, 
management expects flagship smartphone revenue to be flat to down mid-single digit 
QoQ, with flagship SoC launches largely offsetting weakness in other segment. In the 
smart edge segment, MediaTek is looking for mid-to-high single digit growth QoQ, 
driven by new connectivity/auto projects entering mass production. For the PMIC 
business, management expect growth to be flattish QoQ. 
2Q26 revenue beat GS and Street 
2Q26 revenue was NT$152.2bn (2% QoQ/1.2% YoY), above the company’s guidance of 
NT$140.2-$149.2bn, and 5.6%/3.7% higher vs. GSe/cons. 2Q26 GM of 46.2% was

Earnings changes, valuation and risks 
Forecast changes 
We revise up our 2026E-28E earnings by 7%/2%/1% to reflect 1) stronger 2Q26 
operating margin, 2) higher 3Q26 guidance, 3) higher 2026 AI ASIC revenue forecast.

Reiterate Buy; TP raised to NT$7,000 from NT$6,800 
We increase our 12m TP to NT$7,000 from NT$6,800 previously, following our upward 
earnings revisions. Our TP is based on a target P/E multiple of 25x (unchanged; +1.8 stdv 
above 5 yr avg. fwd P/E) applied to our FY2H27E-1H28E EPS (unchanged). Overall, our 
12m TP implies ~97% upside; we reiterate our Buy rating on MediaTek.

### Page 7

Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis - MediaTek 
MediaTek is a leading global IC design house specializing in smartphone AP (application 
processor). We have a positive stance on MediaTek, viewing it as well-positioned to 
transition from a traditional smartphone application provider to an AI-focused vendor, 
beginning with AI smartphones and extending to enterprise ASICs and smart automotive 
solutions (in partnership with NVIDIA) in 2025 and beyond. We expect MediaTek to 
achieve solid multi-year growth, where we expect revenue and earnings to increase by 
43%/59% CAGRs, respectively, in 2025-28E. This growth will be primarily driven by: 1) 
market share gains, particularly in the premium segment (specifically high-end 5G 
flagship SoCs), 2) strong ramp in AI ASIC business, and 3) new TAM in the 
automotive/computing sectors. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - MediaTek (2454.TW) 
Valuation: Our 12m TP of NT$7,000 is based on a target P/E multiple of 25x (1.8 stdv 
above its 5-year trading average) applied to our 2H27E-1H28E EPS. 
Key risks to our views: (1) Weaker-than-expected end demand especially with 
smartphones, (2) Higher foundry cost impacting its margin outlook, (3) Intensifying 
competition that would result in reduced profitability, and (4) Slower ramp in ASIC would 
result in changes in operating leverage

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

M
Update
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Raising  2027 ASIC revenue and
more confident about 2028;
reiterate  OW
Increase in 2027 TPU revenue forecast should still be an upside surprise: The
company now expects it can gain 15%-20% revenue share in 2027 ASIC TAM of US
$80bn (excluding HBM cost). That implies US$12bn-US$16bn revenue, above its
prior outlook for US$7bn-US$12bn. We project 2027 TPU revenue at US$15.7bn.
Because TPU is the major ASIC project in global TAM, MediaTek’s implied TPU

market share could be 20% in 2027, similar to our preview.
Confident in 2nm TPU adopting EMIB-T packaging in 2028: The company is
confident that EMIB-T substrate yield can improve. We asked whether this project
still needs TSMC’s CoWoS capacity as a back-up, and management replied that
“switch to a second source would take time,” which to us means no need for now.
MediaTek also believes its ASIC revenue market share would further increase in
2028—very bullish, in our view.

3Q26 revenue guided to be flat to up 5% Q/Q, better than our down 5% Q/Q: We
cite a sustainable high-end smartphone segment and MediaTek’s chip price hike.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside

n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.
n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

the next few years. Although UMC’s AI revenue contribution remains modest today, management highlighted

revenue to reach US$1bn in 3 years , from US$300m currently.
•Packaging, Testing and AI Infrastructure – AI Revenue Mix Continues to Improve with GM upside.  ASEH

highlighted sustained strength in advanced packaging and testing driven by increasingly complex AI chips, while
continuing to expand capabilities in CoWoS-related packaging, advanced test and next-generation panel-based
technologies. LEAP revenue to be more than US$3.5bn this year and doubling in 2027. Delta Electronics also see
AI revelated revenue to be 25% for this year, higher than their previous expectation of 20%. AI data center
products  will be supported by strong demand for high-efficiency power supplies, power distribution, liquid cooling

and higher rack power density. As AI servers migrate toward increasingly power-intensive rack architectures, Delta
remains one of the key beneficiaries of higher system-level power content.
•ASIC Momentum Accelerates – MediaTek and GUC Expand AI Opportunities. MediaTek reiterated a constructive AI
outlook while highlighting continued expansion across edge AI, automotive and enterprise AI opportunities. The
company has updated its AI ASIC revenue to US$12-16bn (based on 15-20% market share of its US$80bn market

estimate; lift from 10-15% market share; vs our estimate of US$18bn). Mediatek also confirmed that they are
working with second ASIC customer. GUC also continues to benefit from expanding hyperscaler ASIC CPU programs,
with industry discussions pointing to an increasingly robust multi-year design pipeline as cloud providers diversify
compute architectures beyond merchant GPUs, we expect CPU business to doubling next year with revenue
contribution to reach +30%/50% in 2026/27.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

2026.08.03 
洪有辰 C00270@ibfs.com.tw 
3023.TT 信邦 
 用 MAGIC 產業創造更多 MAGIC！

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
信邦成立於 1989 年，總部設立於新北市，為精密連接線束與相關組件大廠，透過醫
療器材、車用航太、綠能環境、工業應用與通訊等五大產業的布局，為全球客戶提供
高附加價值的連接線組服務。近期在機器人、半導體設備與低軌衛星 等也有所著墨。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 365 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作 ，362 
【大盤指數/股價】43120 / 296

➢ 2Q26 工業 、 半導體與機器人等領域小量出貨 ， 產品組合持續優化 
信邦 2Q26 營收達 90.19 億元，QoQ+8.8%、YoY+16.6%，主因綠能、
工業需求回升 ， 且半導體 、 機器人與無人機開始小量出貨 ； 毛利率24.5%
持平上季，雖受低毛利的伺服器機櫃及汽車產品出貨增加衝擊，但高毛
利的客製化工業應用占比提升，抵銷了部分跌幅；營業利益率 10.4%， 
-1.1pts. QoQ，-0.9pts. YoY，主要受新研發、調薪與運費導致費用率上
升影響；業外因匯兌與金融評價利益增加，助攻獲利，2Q26 稅後 EPS 
3.76 元，YoY+19%；累計 1H26 營收 173.11 億元，YoY+8.9%，稅後
EPS 7.37 元，YoY+5%。 
➢ MAGIC 五大應用中，工業占比最大、汽車成長性最高 
信邦在工業應用因人形機器人、智慧交通、半導體設備與AI 基礎建設等
實體 AI 四大核心領域開始迎來收割期，2026 年與 2027 年占比將擴大
至 31.7%與 34%。其中，人形機器人已打入5 家美企，單台線束價值逾
1,500 美元，並結盟正淩與宇隆，預計2026 年及 2027 年出貨 1 萬與 2
萬套 ， 台中新廠2H27 量產後更將加速成長 。 汽車領域則具最高成長性 ，
預估 2026 年與 2027 年增幅達 41.4%與 23.3%。公司策略調整有成：
中國轉向服務 比亞迪(002594.CH)等在地一線新能源車廠；日本市場靠
液冷快充直銷日系大廠；美歐市場則受惠電動車發展，除完成 NACS 充
電線束量產準備，亦積極參展推廣歐規輕量化充電產品。 
➢ 2026~2027 年多項題材陸續發酵 ，調升至買進建議， 目標價365 元 
投研部預估信邦 2026 年/2027 全年營收分別為 361.51 億元/415.97 億
元，YoY+16.5% /15.1%；公司在人形機器人、AI 伺服器機櫃專用的大
電流線束 、 半導體設備與低軌衛星通訊用線束等高毛利產品將陸續出貨 ，
預估毛利率將上升至 24.6%/24.8%；隨著高毛利產品量產後，初期較高
的研發費用將逐漸被抵銷，預估稅後 EPS 分別為 15.19 元/18.16 元。在
評價方面 ， 目前PER 為 19.5X / 16.3X， 過去兩年PER 區間在 14X~26X，
考量公司在台灣與海外所布局的 MAGIC 五大高階應用領域深具潛力，
其中長期 TAM(總潛在市場)仍將處於高速成長階段，信邦將逐步在多地
擴大滲透率，評價有提升的空間，因此調升至買進評等，目標價 365 元
(2027F PER 20X)。

個股報告

公司簡介 
信邦成立於 1989 年 ， 總部設立於台灣新北市汐止區 ， 為高階連接線束與精密連接器組
件的領導大廠 ， 目前已由早期的低毛利消費性電子代工廠 ， 成功轉型為高客製化與高附
加價值的系統整合方案提供商 。 公司為避免單一產業的景氣循環波動 ， 以共同設計與製
造的方式跨足統稱為 「MAGIC」 的五大高階應用領域：1) M (Medical，醫療器材)：高
精密醫療級連接線束 ，應用於斷層掃描 、 超音波與心導管設備等高門檻領域 。醫療線束
對訊號傳輸的零失真與抗干擾(EMI)要求極高，且必需通過生物相容性檢驗，信邦旗下
產品具備耐高溫高壓滅菌與耐重複彎折數十萬次等特性，供應國際一線醫療設備大廠
如 GE 與 Philips 等；2) A (Automotive，車用與航太)：電動車用高壓動力線束、感測
系統、以及全球首張通過 UL 安全認證的 DC 直流快充液冷系統(HPC)。車用線束需長
期暴露於高低溫差 、 劇烈震動與高電壓的環境中 ， 信邦的液冷快充槍透過在線纜內注入
冷卻液，成功在縮小線徑、減輕重量的同時，實現超高功率充電而不發燙，切入歐美地
區的充電基礎設施市場；3) G (Green，綠能環境)：風力發電大型控制櫃線束、太陽能
逆變器連接線等綠色能源基礎設施 。 綠能產品面臨極端氣候的考驗 ， 如離岸風電的鹽霧
腐蝕及陸域風電的極地低溫 ， 信邦除了供應相關線材 ， 也具備大型控制櫃的組裝與測試
能力，為全球風電巨頭如 Vestas (VWS.CO)的長期戰略夥伴，儘管綠能產業近年受到
美國政策波動影響，但在「2050 淨零碳排」的框架下，全球對綠能基礎建設線材依賴
度依然不減；4) I (Industrial，工業應用)、工業自動化機械手臂關節線、半導體整機系
統模組組裝 、 人形機器人全身關鍵線束 、 半導體高階微影與蝕刻設備無塵室線束 。該領
域為信邦近年成長動能最強勁的板塊 ， 在人形機器人應用場景中 ， 機器人全身擁有數十
個靈活的動力關節 ， 需承受極高頻率的扭轉與拉伸， 且空間極度壓縮 ，信邦目前已成功
打入國際人形機器人新創企業供應鏈， 並取得唯一供應商資格 。而在半導體領域 ，公司
也已從零件供應商升級為設備整機組裝企業，已成為國際科技大廠的合作夥伴； 5) C 
(Communication，通訊週邊)：物聯網裝置、雲端資料中心高速微細同軸線、AI 伺服
器液冷散熱模組線組、低軌衛星的地面接收站線材、5G 微基站戶外防水連接線。隨著
AI 伺服器功耗暴增，傳統風扇散熱難以負荷，信邦開發了用於測試與連接散熱器性能
的液冷線組，其 4U 浸沒式微型液冷運算模組與 Dow 進行深度戰略合作。公司同時也
透過旗下子公司睿信電子在太空經濟中也參與了低軌衛星地面接收裝置的高頻連接線
材研發，為 SpaceX(星鏈計畫 Starlink)的供應商。

個股報告 
2Q26 工業、半導體與機器人等領域小量出貨，產品組合持續優化 
信邦 2Q26 營收 90.19 億元，QoQ+8.8%，YoY+16.6%，主因為綠能需求增溫、高附
加價值的工業應用需求持續增溫 ， 再加上半導體設備 、 人形機器人與無人機等領域開始
小量出貨所致。觀察營收細項，綠色能源佔 18.6%、工業應用佔 35.6%、醫療照護佔
9.7%、汽車佔 16.6%、通訊佔 19.5%；毛利率 24.5%，持平於上季，YoY-0.2pts.，受
伺服器機櫃組裝業務比重提升影響，而該業務材料成本占比較高、附加價值相對較低，
加上汽車事業低毛利產品出貨比重增加 ， 對整體毛利率形成壓力 ， 然而工業應用產品線
具備高度客製化及較高的認證門檻 ， 擁有較佳的獲利能力 ， 隨著其營收占比提升 ，部分
抵銷了前述不利因素，使 2Q26 毛利率仍得以維持與 1Q26 相當的水準；營業利益率
10.4%，-1.1pts. QoQ，-0.9pts. YoY，衰退的原因來自公司在機櫃組裝投入較多新產
品研發費用 ， 同時還調整薪資 、 拓展市場以及支付相關運費 ， 導致費用率YoY+0.7pts.；
在營業外損益部分 ， 因匯兌損失減少及金融資產評價利益增加 ，2Q26 合併營業外利益
較去年同期增加 4,743 萬元，成為獲利成長的來源；整體而言，公司在 2Q26 的產品
組合持續優化，稅後 EPS 3.76 元，QoQ+4.4%，YoY+19%。累計 1H26 營收 173.11
億元，YoY+8.9%；累計 1H26 稅後 EPS 7.37 元，YoY+5%。 
圖 2：信邦營收組合

資料來源：信邦年報、國票投顧整理 
MAGIC 五大應用中，工業占比最大、汽車成長性最高 
信邦 MAGIC 五大應用中，2025 年在醫療照護的營收佔比為 8.8%、汽車占比 16.1%、
綠色能源占比 20.3%、工業應用 30.6%、通訊 24.2%。 
展望未來， 投研部預估信邦在 2026 年與 2027 年占比仍然最大 ， 分別達31.7%與 34%，
主因為公司在實體 AI 的四大核心應用即將四件齊發，包含 1)人形機器人：因配線涵蓋
從頭到腳的範圍，單台機器人線束價值超過 1,500 美元，高於消費性電子與部分車款
電動車的單量線束價值， 且毛利率預估介於30%~35%， 為公司下一波的成長動能， 目
前已打入 5 家美系機器人公司，其中甚至成為 Figure 關鍵線束的唯一供應商。為了將
機器人線束與內部機電整合 ， 公司除了結盟轉投資的連接器公司正淩(8147)之外 ， 也與
高階精密金屬加工廠宇隆(2233)簽署合作備忘錄，由信邦負責高靈敏度線束與連接器、
電控模組以及 PCB 板，而宇隆負責 RV 減速機、行星減速機與諧波減速機。目前公司
已邁入少量至中量試產階段 ， 待2H27 台中梧棲新廠完工後加入量產 ， 總體而言 ，2026
年與 2027 年人形機器人線束出貨量預估各為 1 萬與 2 萬套，挹注總營收各為 5 億元
與 10 億元左右，目標於 2028 年挑戰 20 萬套；2)智慧交通與倉儲物流：信邦既有的

### Page 4

- 4 -

個股報告 
工業控制將拓展至多種場景的無人化營運 ， 包含與歐美大廠合作製造自動導引車(AGV)
與自主移動機器人(AMR)專用的高階線材 ， 另外信邦憑藉高階客製化能力也成功將技術
切入商用運輸無人機與電動垂直起降飛行器(eVTOL，俗稱空中計程車)供應鏈，提供具
備航太級安全認證的超輕量、強效抗干擾連接器模組；3)半導體設備：信邦相關產品已
從單純的線材零件延伸至高階機櫃整機組裝，旗下半導體設備客戶從過去的 2 家擴展
至目前的 6 家 ， 包含艾司摩爾(ASML.US)曝光機的超高傳輸線束等 ；4)電源與冷卻等 AI
基礎建設 ： 信邦與全球電力設備模組廠合作 ， 使CSP 大廠在集中式配線櫃(Power Rack)
與匯流排(Busbar Connector Harness) 中採用信邦的超大電流耐高壓主幹線束，另外
還積極切入資料中心中 AI 伺服器與機房的浸沒式液冷連接方案。 
投研部也預估信邦的汽車領域成長性最高， 在 2026 年與 2027 年分別為 41.4%與
23.3%，儘管公司信邦的中國車用產線減少了美系出口外銷，但全面轉向服務比亞迪等
體系的中國在地一線新能源品牌車廠， 專攻中國車廠外銷車款所需的高壓充電 、 電池管
理系統(BMS)及 ADAS 智慧駕駛感測模組線束 ； 公司在日本市場也頗有斬獲 ，自主研發
的直流快充液冷系統順利取得 UL 認證後成功打入日本大型車企 ，同時也透過台灣和中
國產線擴大對日系車廠車載影音與動力控制模組線束的直銷渗透與高度彈性支援；美
國與歐洲市場則受惠於電動車的發展而獲得訂單，信邦已完成對美國客戶 NACS(北美
充電標準)的連接器及高壓充電線束的量產準備，在 2026 年隨著當地基礎設施建設大
量出貨，而歐洲則採取積極的進攻策略，2026 年 6 月首次將電動車充電全系列產品帶
入歐洲 ， 參展慕尼黑Power2Drive Europe 2026 展會 ， 展出專為歐洲開發的歐規Type 
2 交流、CCS2 直流以及 CCS2 液冷充電線與充電插座，升級輕量化的全新設計。 
醫療照護與綠能估成長 17.3%與 11%，通訊產業則小幅衰退 0.9% 
此外，在醫療照護方面，過去佔整體交貨量約 50%的中國產線已經隨美系客戶要求而
移轉至美墨 ， 在俄亥俄州擴建規模達1.5 倍的新廠以及在墨西哥聖路易斯波托西新廠進
行美墨雙廠協作 ， 客製化藥物霧化器與醫用高壓電纜則持續獲歐系客戶積極採購 ， 再加
上供應給日本的高階客製化線束與診斷影像設備相關市佔逐漸增加， 因此預估營收
YoY+17.3%；在綠色能源方面，中國產線的綠能外銷比重在 2026 年因競爭激烈而持
續下降 ， 但日本目前存在小型綠能儲能設備需求 ， 信邦主要配合當地的能源系統整合商
供應高階儲能櫃與分散式微電網的工業級客製化連接線組，在美國墨西哥新廠與美國
俄亥俄州新廠已於近年完工，並在 2026 年步入產能全面放量期，另外公司也受惠於歐
洲風電 大廠 精簡 成本而 在 2026 年獲 得 與 Vestas (VWS.CO)、Siemens Gamesa 
(SGREN)等深化策略結盟的機會，因此預估營收 YoY+11%；在通訊領域方面，信邦正
積極爭取美國企業的低軌衛星地面接收站與陣列天線線束訂單，並針對美國大客戶提
供 AI 資料中心高頻高速傳輸線，惟 2026 年仍處於新舊產品交替期，需待 2027 年營
收才會顯著放大，歐洲通訊訂單主要集中於大型網通設備商的工業級交換機與骨幹網
路連接元件， 因此預估營收 YoY-0.9%。投研部預估 信邦 3Q26 營收 94.98 億元，
QoQ+5.3%，YoY+25.8%；毛利率 24.7%，+0.2pts. QoQ，+1.7pts. YoY；營業利益
率 10.7%，+0.3pts. QoQ，+2.4pts. YoY；稅後淨利率 10%，-0.1pts. QoQ，+0.4pts. 
YoY；稅後 EPS 3.96 元，QoQ+5.3%，YoY+30%。 
2026~2027 年多項題材陸續發酵，調升至買進建議，目標價 365 元

億元，YoY+16.5% /15.1%；公司在人形機器人、AI 伺服器機櫃專用的大電流線束、半
導體設備與低軌衛星通訊用線束等高毛利產品將陸續出貨， 預估毛利率將上升至 24.6% 
/24.8%；隨著高毛利產品量產後，初期較高的研發費用將逐漸被抵銷， 營業利益率預

欣興(3037 TT)  
台灣股市︱電子上游 
投 資 速 報 
買進-維持 目標價 NT$1025 (潛在報酬+43.2%) 2026-07-30

資本支出上修近六成，稼動率維持高檔   
◼ 結 論 與 建 議 
AI 需 求 強 勁 ， 公 司 將 2026 年 資 本 支 出 上 修 ， 加 速 高 階 產 能 布 局 ； ABF 載板
稼 動 率 維 持 90% 以 上 、 高 階 產 能 維 持 滿 載 水 準 ， 支 撐 後 續 營 運 成 長 。 預 估
欣興 (303 7 T T)2025 年 EPS 3.92 元， 2026 年 EPS 23.7 元，預估 2027 年 隨
光復二廠正式量產 、 EMIB 及高階 AI 載 板 出 貨 增 加，產品組合 持續優化 ，EPS 
35. 43 元 ， 建 議 給 予 本 益 比 29 倍 ， 設 定 目 標 價 1025 元（ 29X ＊ 2027(F) ），
評 等 「 買 進 」 。 
風險方面，須留意高階載板原料供應及價格波動，以及 CSP 基於自由現金
流與投資報酬率考量而放緩 AI 資本支出。  
◼ 重點摘要  
大 尺 寸 、高 層 數 與 新 模 組 整 合  
隨 AI 伺 服 器 與 高 效 能 運 算 （ HPC ） 需 求 快 速 擴 張 ， 高 階 ABF 載 板 已 成 為 產
業 主 要 成 長 動 能 。 AI 產 品 持 續 推 動 載 板 朝 大 尺 寸 、 高 層 數 與 模 組 整 合 方 向
升級，帶動高階材料需求提升並凸顯供應鏈瓶頸 。 在 AI 產品呈現「一年一
代 」的 快 速 演 進 節 奏 下 ， 有利具備技術與量產能力的台日載板廠維持競爭優
勢。市場 預期 2026 年 高 階 ABF 供 需 仍 偏 緊 ， 2027 年隨 AI GPU 、 AI ASIC
與 大 型 封 裝 需 求 進 一 步 放 量 ， 緊 張 態 勢 可 能 延 續 。 同 時 ， CPO 等 新 世 代 高
速光電整合架構逐步受到關注，未來可望帶動載板 在 高 速 傳 輸 、 散 熱 與 系 統
整合設計上 的 技 術 升 級 。
